 Run overview   ([default] = not set by you)

  Build          : UNINSTRUMENTED (RVV_INSTRUMENTED=0) -- comparable to the reference design   [fixed at build time: make INSTRUMENTED=]
                   per-tile timers and perf counters compiled out; pack/compute breakdown = nan
  Protocol       : REFERENCE (matches test_gemm.c:339-361)   [default]
  Warm-up        : none: the cold first rep is inside the mean
  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]
  Variants       : MR8_NR4   [config.json]
  Cache blocking : analytical model (get_optim_mc_nc_kc)   [default]
                   nc overrides: OFF (every shape uses the heuristic's nc; config.json has: none)   [--no-nc-overrides]
                   RVV_FORCE_NC: not set   [default]
  Dataset        : ../../datasets/yolo_correctness_small.dat
  Logs           : logs/raw_fp32_AXPY_FP32_Pack_noinstr_refproto_scalarpack_no_nc_override.txt

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/yolo_correctness_small.dat

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Protocol       : REFERENCE (matches test_gemm.c:339-361)   [default]
  Warm-up        : none: the cold first rep is inside the mean
  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]
  Variants       : MR8_NR24   [config.json]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Protocol       : REFERENCE (matches test_gemm.c:339-361)   [default]
  Warm-up        : none: the cold first rep is inside the mean
  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]
  Variants       : MR16_NR6   [config.json]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Protocol       : REFERENCE (matches test_gemm.c:339-361)   [default]
  Warm-up        : none: the cold first rep is inside the mean
  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]
  Variants       : MR16_NR10   [config.json]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/yolo_correctness_small.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md


  [YC_m300_n4_k9 (300x4x9)] Running scalar reference ...   [YC_m300_n4_k9 (300x4x9)] Testing variant 1/1 : MR8_NR4                                                                                         +- YC_m300_n4_k9 (300x4x9) 8x4 [MR8_NR4: 8 rows x 4 columns of C per microkernel tile]
  |  Speed [GOPs = billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; higher is better]

  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

 Run overview   ([default] = not set by you)
============================================================
  Build          : UNINSTRUMENTED (RVV_INSTRUMENTED=0) -- comparable to the reference design   [fixed at build time: make INSTRUMENTED=]
                   per-tile timers and perf counters compiled out; pack/compute breakdown = nan
  Protocol       : REFERENCE (matches test_gemm.c:339-361)   [default]
  Warm-up        : none: the cold first rep is inside the mean
  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]
  Variants       : MR8_NR16   [config.json]
  Cache blocking : analytical model (get_optim_mc_nc_kc)   [default]
                   nc overrides: OFF (every shape uses the heuristic's nc; config.json has: none)   [--no-nc-overrides]
                   RVV_FORCE_NC: not set   [default]
  Dataset        : ../../datasets/yolo_correctness_small.dat
  Logs           : logs/raw_fp32_AXPY_FP32_Pack_noinstr_refproto_scalarpack_no_nc_override.txt

                   logs/reps_fp32_AXPY_FP32_Pack_noinstr_refproto_scalarpack_no_nc_override.txt (every timed rep, in the order it ran)   [--save-reps]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]

   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]

  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

 Run overview   ([default] = not set by you)
============================================================
  Build          : UNINSTRUMENTED (RVV_INSTRUMENTED=0) -- comparable to the reference design   [fixed at build time: make INSTRUMENTED=]
                   per-tile timers and perf counters compiled out; pack/compute breakdown = nan
  Protocol       : REFERENCE (matches test_gemm.c:339-361)   [default]
  Warm-up        : none: the cold first rep is inside the mean
  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]
  Variants       : MR16_NR10   [config.json]
  Cache blocking : analytical model (get_optim_mc_nc_kc)   [default]
                   nc overrides: OFF (every shape uses the heuristic's nc; config.json has: none)   [--no-nc-overrides]
                   RVV_FORCE_NC: not set   [default]
  Dataset        : ../../datasets/yolo_correctness_small.dat
  Logs           : logs/raw_fp32_AXPY_FP32_Pack_noinstr_refproto_scalarpack_no_nc_override.txt

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/yolo_correctness_small.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

 Run overview   ([default] = not set by you)
============================================================
  Build          : UNINSTRUMENTED (RVV_INSTRUMENTED=0) -- comparable to the reference design   [fixed at build time: make INSTRUMENTED=]
                   per-tile timers and perf counters compiled out; pack/compute breakdown = nan
  Protocol       : REFERENCE (matches test_gemm.c:339-361)   [default]
  Warm-up        : none: the cold first rep is inside the mean
  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]
  Variants       : MR16_NR14   [config.json]
  Cache blocking : analytical model (get_optim_mc_nc_kc)   [default]
                   nc overrides: OFF (every shape uses the heuristic's nc; config.json has: none)   [--no-nc-overrides]
                   RVV_FORCE_NC: not set   [default]
  Dataset        : ../../datasets/yolo_correctness_small.dat
  Logs           : logs/raw_fp32_AXPY_FP32_Pack_noinstr_refproto_scalarpack_no_nc_override.txt

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/yolo_correctness_small.dat

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Build          : UNINSTRUMENTED (RVV_INSTRUMENTED=0) -- comparable to the reference design   [fixed at build time: make INSTRUMENTED=]
                   per-tile timers and perf counters compiled out; pack/compute breakdown = nan
  Protocol       : REFERENCE (matches test_gemm.c:339-361)   [default]
  Warm-up        : none: the cold first rep is inside the mean
  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]
  Variants       : MR24_NR8   [config.json]
  Cache blocking : analytical model (get_optim_mc_nc_kc)   [default]
                   nc overrides: OFF (every shape uses the heuristic's nc; config.json has: none)   [--no-nc-overrides]
                   RVV_FORCE_NC: not set   [default]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  Reps           : until tmin = 0.2 s has passed, one clock read per rep   [--tmin]
  Correctness    : checked against the scalar reference GEMM, AFTER timing   [skip_ref_run is false in config.json]
  Packing        : scalar packing forced   [--scalar-pack]
  Pack lines     : not cleared: each line the packer writes is read from the L2 first   [default]
  Pad ldb        : ldb = k (no padding)   [default]
  Pack B         : row (shipped xDSL packer)   [default]
  Kernel family  : fp32 AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN 256 | arch jbpi2   [config.json]

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/yolo_correctness_small.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [the whole GEMM call per rep -- pack A and B + microkernel compute + loop overhead; same region the reference design times]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md
